P183

Summary

/tmp/claude-1040/-home-ftk3187-github-PSED/f11a7f05-73cc-4485-aad2-d984ea626b83/scratchpad/p183/summary.txt

P183: read-only copies of the form and prompt text behind three causes. 0 calls. No file changed except kb/reports/P183.html (every table
below was written to a scratch folder). Texts are copied verbatim; no fix, no proposal.

Cause 1, pass 0
1a. The pass-0 instruction as pass0_paper.instruction() writes it now (TERMS from kb/pilot/lists.py, UNITS, the layouts); the definitions
    of run, specimen, simulation, model run (TERMS) and of recipe / measurement activity (CODEBOOK.md section a, which the pass-0 prompt
    carries whole); every sentence on how many units to list or when two things are one unit; the unit fields of form_pass0.json with
    the instruction sentence that names each (name_as_printed: no sentence names it). The schema has no descriptions; role is an enum.
1b. The pass-0 request: instruction, CODEBOOK.md whole, EXAMPLE (render.example), TABLES (markdown + cell ids), FIGURES (captions, panel
    ids, every figures.json curve with its series label and conditions, cells, peaks, sets, derived), ELEMENT INDEX (every element: id,
    kind, locator), PAPER (document.md whole). The request cannot be rebuilt by the code as it is now: render.example walks the papers in
    reverse index order and reads wer21's gold (10.1116_6.0001094.facts.csv), which does not exist, so the prompt hashes of yli18's
    pass-0 line 122 (2026-10-20) and gao15's line 2 (2026-10-24) are not compared. The element index of both papers, in order.
1c. gao15: pass 0 2 runs + 4 specimens; gold 9 runs + 14 specimens (render.gold_units); paired by render.pair_units: 4 specimens matched,
    the 2 runs merged+split with 2 gold runs, 7 gold runs and 10 gold specimens missing. mat22: pass 0 42 runs (no specimen); gold 54
    runs + 54 specimens; 34 runs matched, 8 pass-0 runs extra, 20 gold runs and 54 gold specimens missing.
1d. 184 of the 340 measurement activities are <entity>:meas:measurement (no method; the method column of a measurement activity is
    empty in all 340, the method is in its id). compile.measurement (kb/extract/compile.py:1639-1642) keeps a method only when it is
    in facts.MEAS_METHODS (kb/pilot/facts.py:117); 11 ontology measurement methods are not in it (epma, eqp, ftir, giwaxs,
    infrared_microscopy, oes, sims, tem_eds, tof_erda, uv_vis_spectroscopy, xrr). Their 435 child facts: 284 figures.json curves or values
    (compile.py:2223 makes their measurement with method ''; no figures.json curve or value has a method field), 119 pass-1 records
    (method calculation 53, giwaxs 16, epma 10, tof_erda 10, empty 8, tabulated 7, ...), 32 figure images (compile.py:1817: no
    measurement method among the caption's methods). Pass 0 gave the figures.json ids the data role measured for 145 rows, none for 139.

Cause 2, step and holds
2a. CODEBOOK.md now: rule 1 (step), the pulse_time / purge_time / exposure rows, the step row and its eight value rows (P181); the two
    step EXAMPLES of the yli18 and of the gre23 prompt as step_examples() writes them now. At the gre23 s9.p4 request (2026-10-24
    04:27, form P163) the last CODEBOOK commit (3c56a146) had rule 1 and the step row without the value rows; step_examples() did not
    exist (P181).
2b. gre23 s9.p4 (records line 4, group s9+s10+s11): the element as the section group shows it and its 12 records. No record holds
    the 1920 s (32 min) H2O hold as a value; the quote '1920 s (32 min) \text{H}_2\text{O} hold, 2880 s (48 min) purge' is on a
    purge_time record of 2880 s with step purge_B.
2c. compile.Run.step_fill (P181) and the 35 rows it filled (P181 section 5), each with its record's quote: no quote contains hold,
    static or held.

Cause 3, qualifiers
3a. Every sentence of the pass-1 INTRO / RULES and CODEBOOK.md that names a qualifier or a qualifier key, now, marked present or not
    at the last commits before the yli18 s8.p24 request (line 123, 2026-10-20, no form version) and the gre23 s14.p6 request (line 6,
    2026-10-24, P163); the rows that exist only at those commits. The qualifier block of the provider's record schema now
    (pass1_elements.flat_schema): 'qualifiers' is a required field of the record (nullable), no key inside it is required,
    additionalProperties absent; the same block from form.json at each line's commit.
3b. Over the thirteen papers' compiled facts: stat 55 without an uncertainty field, 3 with; between 39 / 2; n 39 / 1; x 11, all on
    text records, none on a figure-derived fact (points_ref).

1a. The pass-0 instruction (pass0_paper.instruction(), verbatim)

/tmp/claude-1040/-home-ftk3187-github-PSED/f11a7f05-73cc-4485-aad2-d984ea626b83/scratchpad/p183/1a_instruction.txt

You read one ALD paper and list its experiment units, the layout of each of its tables and the layout of each figure panel.

TERMS (from the codebook):
- run: one deposition the paper performs. A run exists where the paper STATES a deposition — in the experimental section, a caption ("deposited at … using … cycles", "films grown on glassy carbon"), a table row, a grid point. A stated deposition is a run even when no data of it is in the figures; data without a stated deposition is not a run. Specimens coated together share one run; separate depositions are separate runs even with the same chemistry, temperature and structure.
- specimen: one physical sample that is measured (a coated structure, a film, a substrate piece); it comes from one run.
- input entity: a thing that exists before any deposition (a substrate, a porous body), placed under the recipe and named in a deposition activity's inputs.
- simulation: any model the paper applies to produce a computed value or curve — a numerical simulation, or an analytical equation fitted to or evaluated on the data; each evaluation with its own inputs is a model run (gor03's theory, art19's slope relation, gre23's eq 1–2). The paper's own model and a model it compares against are separate simulations.
- model run: one set of inputs of a simulation whose result the paper reports. Two solution methods or approximations of the same inputs are one model run.
- fitted or simulated curve of a specimen: a curve fitted or simulated to match a measured specimen is a result of that specimen, not a model run.
- series / replicate / grid (comparisons): never a parent and never asked of the extractor; the compiler derives them from the condition vectors of the experiment units — series: members differ in exactly one quantity; replicate: in none; grid: in two. A name the authors give such a set ("Series A") is kept as its label.

UNITS: one item per run, specimen, simulation and model run.
- role: run, specimen, simulation or model_run; parent: a specimen's run, a model run's simulation; null otherwise.
- list every run even when several share all conditions; list a shared run once when the paper says the samples were coated together.
- evidence_quote (every unit, never null) is copied from the paper: the words that state the deposition of a run, that name a
  specimen, a simulation or a model run.
- parent: never null on a specimen (its run) or a model run (its simulation).
- data: the figures.json ids (FIGURES below: curve, cell and peak ids) whose results belong to this unit, each with its role from
  the caption: measured (read from a specimen), fitted (a line fitted or simulated to match that specimen's measurement) or
  simulated (a model run's result); null when none.
- a unit established by a table row or a grid point: introduced_in = that row's name cell / that cell id, evidence_quote = the
  cell's text.
- introduced_in: the element ids where the unit is named, then every table or figure element that shows it (ELEMENT INDEX).
- structure_class, specimen_class, experiment_kind: only values of the form's enums, only when the paper states them.
- recipe fields of a run (P181): reactant_A, reactant_B, carrier_gas (species ids) and process_mode (one of the form's values), each only
  when the paper states it for that run, with <field>_quote copied from the paper (the words that state it); null otherwise, never
  inferred. A simulation or model run: the same fields when the paper states them for the model, else null.

TABLE LAYOUTS (one per table of TABLES below):
- row_per_unit: Each row is one experiment unit (a run, a specimen or a model run); the columns are its conditions and results.
- not_units: The rows are not experiment units: a symbol table (symbol, meaning, value) or a comparison with the literature (cited values).
- other: none of these; describe the layout (description).
- unit_name_cells: the cell ids that name the units; units: the unit ids in the order the table gives them.

PANEL LAYOUTS (one per figure panel of FIGURES below):
- curve_per_unit: Each curve is one experiment unit; the panel compares units that differ in the conditions the legend names. Example: one curve per model run (Ylilammi Fig. 4).
- point_per_unit: Each point or grid cell is one experiment unit; the axes are its conditions, the value or colour its result. Each grid point or scatter point is one unit; list every point as a run (or specimen) with its coordinates as its evidence (P145). Example: a heat map whose grid points are runs (axes = conditions, colour = result).
- panel_one_unit: The whole panel is one experiment unit; its curves are several results of one measurement or one model run: a panel whose curves are several results of one unit — a measured and a fitted profile, scans per element (P145; P146: the solution-method sentence lives in TERMS model run only). Example: an EDS panel = one specimen, its curves one measurement's results per element.
- other: none of these; describe the layout (description).
- unit: the one unit of a panel_one_unit panel; axis_quantities and value_quantity: the quantity ids of a point_per_unit panel.

Every field is present; null when it does not apply. Answer with JSON matching the form.

    def measurement(self, ent, method):
        """<ent>:meas:<method>; P148 (C1): "measurement" when the method is not in facts.MEAS_METHODS."""
        method = method if method in F.MEAS_METHODS else ""
        i = f"{ent}:meas:{method or 'measurement'}"

2b. gre23 s9.p4: the element as sent

/tmp/claude-1040/-home-ftk3187-github-PSED/f11a7f05-73cc-4485-aad2-d984ea626b83/scratchpad/p183/2b_element.txt

gre23 s9.p4, records line 4 (pass 1, group s9+s10+s11, form version P163, request 2026-10-24T04:27:34+00:00); the element as a section group shows it (render.group: 'id: text', markup stripped):

s9.p4: Diethylzinc (DEZ, min. 95%, Strem Chemicals) and de-ionized  $\text{H}_2\text{O}$  were used as ALD precursors. Both were contained in stainless steel cylinders heated to 37 °C with vapor flow rates controlled by metering valves. Infiltration of the NPCs was accomplished via static dosing; that is, precursor vapor was held in the deposition chamber under zero-flow conditions for an extended time to allow diffusion into the NPC. An ALD run consisted of 20 cycles, and a cycle consisted of the following  $\sim 160$  min sequence: 10–14 s DEZ pulse, 1920 s (32 min) DEZ hold, 2880 s (48 min) purge, 8–11 s  $\text{H}_2\text{O}$  pulse, 1920 s (32 min)  $\text{H}_2\text{O}$  hold, 2880 s (48 min) purge. The DEZ and  $\text{H}_2\text{O}$  pulse times were varied manually to improve the consistency of the exposure pressures, which were generally  $\sim 9$  Torr but inevitably fluctuated, possibly due to imperfect electronic valve control and/or variation in the temperature of small unheated portions of the precursor lines (see Fig. S2 in the supplementary material44). During pulse and hold steps, the chamber isolation valve was closed. During hold steps, ALD valves were also closed to isolate the chamber from the precursor

2c. compile.Run.step_fill, verbatim

/tmp/claude-1040/-home-ftk3187-github-PSED/f11a7f05-73cc-4485-aad2-d984ea626b83/scratchpad/p183/2c_code.txt

kb/extract/compile.py lines 1130-1159 (Run.step_fill, P181; unchanged since):

    def step_fill(self, recs):
        """P181 (user), every form version: the definitional step of a setting or observation of pulse_time, purge_time or exposure whose
        step is empty, from its run's recipe fields (the recipe fill): its species = the run's reactant_A -> reactant_A (pulse_time,
        exposure) / purge_A (purge_time); = reactant_B -> reactant_B / purge_B, and for a run of process_mode plasma a pulse_time of its
        reactant_B -> plasma. The record's run: its about unit (a run or a simulation), a specimen's parent run, a model run's simulation;
        about 'paper': the paper's runs, when every one naming the species gives it the same role. No species, no run, the run's fields
        null, a species matching neither, runs disagreeing: the step stays empty (a log line 'a1: step fill ...'). A step set is untouched."""
        units = {u["id"]: u for u in self.pass0 if isinstance(u, dict) and not u.get("origin")}
        runs = [u for u in units.values() if u.get("role") in ("run", "simulation")]
        for r in recs:
            rec = r["rec"]
            q = rec.get("qualifiers") if isinstance(rec.get("qualifiers"), dict) else {}
            if r.get("origin") or rec.get("type") not in ("setting", "observation") or rec.get("quantity") not in C.X.STEP_QUANTITIES or q.get("step"):
                continue
            sp, ab = q.get("species") or "", rec.get("about") if isinstance(rec.get("about"), str) else ""
            u = units.get(ab)
            cand = runs if ab == "paper" else [] if not u else [u] if u.get("role") in ("run", "simulation") else [units[u["parent"]]] if u.get("parent") in units else []
            roles = {("A", "") if sp == x.get("reactant_A") else ("B", x.get("process_mode") or "") for x in cand if sp and sp in (x.get("reactant_A"), x.get("reactant_B"))}
            step, why = "", ("no species" if not sp else f"no run (about {ab or '-'})" if not cand else
                             "run fields null" if not any(x.get("reactant_A") or x.get("reactant_B") for x in cand) else
                             "species matches neither field" if not roles else "runs disagree on the species' role" if len(roles) > 1 else "")
            if not why:
                half, mode = next(iter(roles))
                step = (f"purge_{half}" if rec["quantity"] == "purge_time" else
                        "plasma" if half == "B" and mode == "plasma" and rec["quantity"] == "pulse_time" else f"reactant_{half}")
                rec["qualifiers"] = {**q, "step": step}
                why = "the run's recipe field"
            self.count("a1", f"step fill: {step or 'empty'} ({why})")
            self.note(f"a1: step fill {r['eid']} {rec['quantity']} {rec.get('value')} species {sp or '-'} about {ab or '-'}: {step or '(empty)'} ({why})")
        return recs
